- **Numbering has no shipped series.** Series are admin data created through the API; each document module (M2 onwards) registers the default series its document types need when it arrives, so a fresh tenant has no series until then. Document types are free codes until the document registry exists.
- **Numbering roles.** No shipped role template grants `numbering.*` yet; the owner/admin wildcard covers it. Role templates gain numbering grants with the accounting roles in M2. The same holds for `platform.*` (jobs and schedules).
- **Outbox archive is a delete.** Published messages and their inbox rows older than 30 days are deleted by `ops.outbox_archive`; the archive partition of ADR-0010 arrives when reporting needs event history.
- **A dead letter blocks its aggregate** until an operator retries it or discards it with a reason (strict per-aggregate order). Operators are made with the migrator (`operator --email`), not through the API; operator actions on the outbox are kept on the message and in the log, not in a workspace's audit chain (A-128).
- **Embedded worker is off by default** (`Quicker:Worker:Embedded=false`); `make up` runs the separate `worker` service. The worker image is not built in CI (only the solution is built and tested there).
- **Job progress is polled**, not pushed: the server-sent events channel for progress arrives with the web shell (1.10).
- **Idempotency-Key is optional on the server**: the web client sends one on every write since the post-M4 routine pass (and resends a write once under the same key when the network loses its answer), but integrations and scripts may not yet, so `Quicker:Api:Idempotency:Required` stays `false` by default (A-135). Responses above 1 MB, files, and 401/403 refusals are not stored (a replay re-executes); a replay carries the status, body and content type but not other headers (`Location`, `ETag`).
- **Webhook filters are applied** since the post-M4 routine pass: `aggregateType`, or any top-level field of the event payload (such as `companyId`), all of them must match (text comparison, letter case ignored); nested fields and lists of values are not filterable yet. The event catalogue section of the OpenAPI document (AsyncAPI-style) is part of 1.9.
- **Outbound requests to tenant-chosen addresses reach the public internet only** (webhook receivers, single sign-on providers): internal networks an operator wants reachable are listed in `Quicker:Outbound:AllowedPrivateNetworks`. These clients connect directly, never through an HTTP proxy, so a deployment whose egress must go through a proxy cannot deliver webhooks yet (A-127). The exchange-rate providers and the breached-password check call fixed, operator-configured hosts and are not restricted.
- **The web shell covers the admin screens of 1.4–1.8 at v1 depth**: calendars, units, settings, SoD rules and exceptions, API keys, SSO connections and the sign-in policy have screens since the post-M4 routine pass; comments, files and history have a tab on purchase orders, supplier invoices, requests for quotation, landed costs, suppliers, items and journals, and Discussion and History toggles under the details of requisitions, receipts, returns, blanket agreements, supplier payments, payment proposals, stock adjustments, transfers, counts, assemblies and revaluations, since the post-M4 routine pass; every audit event opens in full. Assignments limited to several companies, branches and warehouses, the network allow-list, SSO group-to-role maps and revoking an SoD exception have screens too (A-126). The daily accounting routines keep a run log per company (Recurring & deferrals → Run history). Permission descriptions come from the code-defined catalogue in English (the keys and every label around them are bilingual). Members who sign in only through SSO have no password to step up with; they sign in again (step-up through the identity provider is a later slice). Forms are controlled React state, not TanStack Form yet; the grid exports what it shows (A-129) but has no inline editing, grouping or pinning yet (data grid v2 with the first document lists). The company form sends the posting policies it does not show (defaults on create, unchanged on edit) because the contract marks them required. The 100k-row grid performance budget is met by virtualization but not yet measured in CI.
- **The shell is responsive** (top menu bar; a drawer under 1024px; 44px touch targets); the mobile scanning app of 3.8 runs in it (see its boundaries above).

## How to run what exists

```
# Docker only, any OS (the README's "Run it"): docker compose up -d postgres minio mailpit; docker compose run --rm migrator;
#   docker compose up -d api worker web; then http://localhost:5173 as owner@quicker.example / DemoPass2026!.
#   docker compose run --rm migrator demo rebuilds the demo tenant; QUICKER_API_PORT in .env moves the API off 8080.
#   The trader program on its own (zero data, its own database and ports 5174/8090): docker compose -f docker-compose.trader.yml up -d --build;
#   on Windows deploy\windows\setup-trader.bat once (ADR-0035 §19).
# prerequisites for the targets below: .NET 10 SDK, Node 22 + pnpm, PostgreSQL 16+ (or Docker for `make up`)
make migrate            # applies db/migrations and db/repeatable to the local database
make test-dotnet        # 253 tests: kernel, schema contract, RLS isolation, append-only, migration replay, identity API, audit API and chain integrity, organization API, numbering API, outbox/jobs/scheduler, webhooks and idempotency, notifications/email, attachments and storage, comments/activities/links/views/custom fields rate limits, filter/paging/ETags, tracing and health, the demo seeder, charts of accounts, the posting engine, journals and routines, period control, GL inquiry, the invariant harness, the item master and unit conversions, the workflow engine, the supplier master, purchasing from requisition to order, goods receipts, supplier invoices, landed costs, supplier returns, the payables subledger, supplier payments, supplier intelligence, document search, the customer master, commission plans, the pipeline, CRM activities and the Customer 360 with its timing on the demo tenant (the S3 test runs when QUICKER_TEST_S3_ENDPOINT points at MinIO)
pnpm install && pnpm -r test && pnpm --filter @quicker/tools check-diagrams   # includes the design system's axe suite
make demo               # rebuilds the demo tenant (migrate + seed first, about half a minute): 3 companies with charts, posting profiles, dimensions and a year of posted books (332 journals, closed periods, a correction, the harness green); sign in as owner@quicker.example / DemoPass2026!
make backup             # pg_dump of the whole database plus a manifest (row counts in the dump's snapshot, SHA-256) under backups/; OUT=file to choose
make restore FROM=backups/<file>.dump DB=<new name>   # restores into a new database only, compares every table with the manifest, runs the invariant harness
make verify             # the invariant harness over every active tenant (TENANT=slug for one); runbook: docs/operations/backup-and-restore.md
make web                # http://localhost:5173 against the API on 8080 (sign in to the demo tenant or sign up a workspace, then explore)
E2E_API_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8080 pnpm --filter @quicker/web e2e   # Playwright journeys in EN and AR with axe (API must be running): admin journeys, the settings journeys (units and conversions, calendars, settings, SoD, API keys, SSO with a group mapped to a role, sign-in policy with allowed networks, and a real step-up after a one-minute window), the access journeys (roles designed from the catalogue and a template, a blocked combination, a member given a role limited to two companies and an acknowledged warning, the conflict excepted and the exception revoked, a role taken away), the transfer journey (35 of 40 shipped, 20 received with 5 lost in transit, the rest received), the accounting tools journey (chart CSV round trip, statutory mapping, journal import, an attachment), the item structure journey (an assembly's BOM versions and explosion, variants, a substitute, a picture), the journal lines journey (a cost centre and a supplier on a journal's lines, refused at posting without them, kept through an edit, posted, read in Arabic), the document search journey (a receipt and its order found by number from the palette, the receipt opened from its journal entry and its audit event, the palette in Arabic), the record discussion journey (a comment with a mention, a reply, an edit and a removal; the item's history and the audit event in full; the explorer filtered, narrowed to the record and read in Arabic), the accounting journey, the inventory journey (M3 web pass), the approvals journey (4.0: a definition checked, activated, an adjustment routed and shown with its why panel), the supplier journey (4.1: terms previewed, a supplier with its group terms, contact, encrypted bank account, registration and hold), the purchasing journey (4.2–4.6: a requisition ordered, an order submitted, sent, changed, received from its own button and invoiced from the receipt's, a landed cost posted, two units returned at their landed cost and credited by a debit note started from the return and applied to the invoice, the order's smart buttons counted and followed, an RFQ compared and awarded, a blanket agreement activated), the account journeys (profile, an authenticator app, a security key registered and used in Chromium's virtual authenticator, a recovery code used once, sessions, a password change, the page in Arabic; a workspace requiring two-step verification enrolling its owner at sign-in; a key-only person confirming a sensitive action with the key after a one-minute window; on localhost), the payables journey (4.7: a bank account, an invoice paid in part with a remainder on account, the remainder applied, aging, the supplier's statement, a proposal approved and paid), the sales journey (5.1: a customer group, a commission plan tried before use, a rep, a customer with a credit limit and a contact, a deal moved from its 360 and on the board, lost with its reason, another won, an activity done with its outcome, a credit hold; the screens in Arabic), the pricing journey (5.2: a standard list with breaks, a derived wholesale list for a customer, a buy-ten-get-one promotion, a floor, the price check with "why this price", a typed discount under the floor; the screens in Arabic)
E2E_API_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8080 pnpm --filter @quicker/web e2e --project mobile   # the scanning journeys (3.8, 4.3) on an emulated phone: bin, barcode, quantity, offline queue, Arabic, receiving against an order; `--project chromium` runs only the desktop journeys
# items: GET/POST /api/v1/items (?q=&filter=&limit=&cursor=), GET /items/{id}?expand=uoms,variants,suppliers,substitutes, GET /items/by-code/{code}, GET /items/by-barcode/{barcode}
#   GET  /api/v1/items/{id}/convert?quantity=2&from=CTN&to=DZ  (exact or refused), PUT /items/{id}/uoms, POST /items/{id}/barcodes, POST /items/{id}/variants, POST /items/{id}/suppliers
#   PUT  /api/v1/items/{id}/company-settings/{companyId}, PUT /items/{id}/warehouse-settings/{warehouseId}, PUT /items/{id}/substitutes, PUT /items/{id}/image
#   POST /api/v1/items/{id}/boms {kind, outputQty, lines:[{componentItemCode, quantity, uom, scrapPct}]}, GET /items/boms/{bomId}/explode?quantity=3
#   GET  /api/v1/items/export (CSV), POST /items/import (text/csv or {items:[...]}), /items/categories, /items/brands, /items/attributes
# inventory: GET/POST /api/v1/inventory/warehouses (?companyId=), PUT /inventory/warehouses/{id}, GET/POST /inventory/warehouses/{id}/bins, PUT/DELETE .../bins/{binId}
#   GET  /api/v1/inventory/stock?companyId=&q=&warehouseId=&onlyAvailable=   (global stock search, paged)
#   GET  /api/v1/inventory/stock/balances?companyId=&itemId=&warehouseId=&includeZero=   GET /inventory/stock/availability?companyId=&itemId=&warehouseId=
#   GET  /api/v1/inventory/stock/ledger?companyId=&itemId=&warehouseId=&from=&to=&sourceDocumentType=&sourceDocumentId=&limit=&cursor=
#   POST /api/v1/inventory/reservations {companyId, itemId, warehouseId, quantity, sourceDocumentType, sourceDocumentId, expiresAt}, POST /inventory/reservations/{id}/release
#   POST /api/v1/inventory/transfers {companyId, fromWarehouseId, toWarehouseId, lines:[{itemId, quantity, uom}]}, PUT /inventory/transfers/{id}, POST .../ship, POST .../receive {lines:[{lineId, quantity}]} (empty = the rest), POST .../cancel
#   costing: GET /api/v1/inventory/costing/item-cost?companyId=&itemId=&warehouseId=&asOf=   GET /inventory/costing/valuation?companyId=&asOf=&warehouseId=&itemId=&includeZero=
#   GET  /api/v1/inventory/costing/entries/{sleId}  (why did this cost change)   GET /inventory/costing/runs?companyId=&itemId=&status=   GET /inventory/costing/runs/{id}   POST .../runs/{id}/run
#   GET/POST /api/v1/inventory/costing/standard-costs {companyId, itemId, standardCost, effectiveFrom, reason}

#   GET/POST /api/v1/inventory/revaluations {companyId, kind: nrv_writedown|manual, postingDate, lines:[{itemCode, warehouseId, newUnitCost}]}, PUT .../{id}, POST .../{id}/post|cancel

#   transfers: kind one_step|two_step on POST; receive lines may carry {shortage, shortageReasonCode, shortageNote}
#   lots: GET /api/v1/inventory/lots?itemId=&status=&expiringBefore=&q=   GET /inventory/lots/suggest?companyId=&itemId=&warehouseId=&quantity=   POST/PUT /inventory/lots, POST /inventory/lots/{id}/status {status, reason, recallReference}, GET /inventory/lots/{id}/trace
#   serials: GET /api/v1/inventory/serials?itemId=&status=&warehouseId=&lotId=&q=   GET /inventory/serials/by-number?itemId=&serialNumber=   POST /inventory/serials/{id}/status {status: in_repair|in_stock, note}   GET /inventory/serials/{id}/history
#   stock lines (documents and the engine) take lotNumber/expiresOn/serialNumbers/partnerId; adjustments, assemblies and transfers carry them on their lines

#   PUT /workflow/definitions/{id} (draft in place; active → next draft version), POST .../activate | /retire

#   modules: IWorkflowEngine.SubmitAsync(subject) | RaiseBlockAsync(block) | ConsumeOverrideAsync(kind, entityType, entityId) | CancelAsync; register an IWorkflowSubjectProvider per entity type

#   GET /partners/{id}/supplier-accounts; PUT .../supplier-accounts/{companyId} {supplierGroupId, paymentTermsId, deliveryTermsId, postingGroupId, whtCodeId, currency, leadTimeDays, priceTolerancePct, qtyTolerancePct, requiresPo, isActive}; POST .../hold {status: purchase|payment|all, reason} | .../release
#   GET /api/v1/partners/suppliers?companyId=&q=&holdStatus=held; GET/POST/PUT /partners/supplier-groups | /payment-terms (+ POST /payment-terms/{id}/schedule {companyId, invoiceDate, amount, currency, deliveryDate?}) | /delivery-terms | /wht-codes
# web screens: /accounting/chart, /accounting/journals, /accounting/trial-balance, /accounting/ledger, /accounting/journal-entries, /accounting/periods (g h / g u / g t / g l / g e / g p); /approvals (g 1), /workflows (g 2); /purchasing/suppliers (g 3), /purchasing/settings (g 4)

make api                # http://localhost:8080/health/ready and /api/v1/openapi.json
make observe            # everything plus Grafana/Tempo/Prometheus/Loki on http://localhost:3000 (admin/admin); GET /health/deps lists every check
make api-contract       # rebuilds contracts/openapi-v1.json (OpenAPI 3.1) and apps/web/src/api/schema.d.ts; commit both
# sign up a workspace: POST /api/v1/auth/signup {tenantName, slug, ownerEmail, ownerName, password}
# audit: GET /api/v1/audit/events, GET /api/v1/audit/records/{type}/{id}, POST /api/v1/audit/chain/verify, POST /api/v1/audit/chain/anchor
# organization: POST /api/v1/organization/companies {code, legalName:{en,ar}, country, functionalCurrency, timeZone, fiscalCalendarId?}

#   POST /api/v1/organization/rates {rateType:"spot", fromCurrency:"USD", toCurrency:"IQD", validFrom:"2026-09-01", rate:1310}

#   POST /api/v1/organization/rates/import {provider:"ecb"|"openexchangerates"}
#   GET  /api/v1/organization/companies/{id}/working-days?from=2026-09-24&days=1  (due date: Thursday + 1 → Sunday in Iraq)
#   POST /api/v1/organization/dimension-sets {values:{COST_CENTER: valueId, PROJECT: valueId}}; GET /api/v1/organization/uom-conversions/convert?from=&to=&value=
# numbering: POST /api/v1/numbering/series {code, documentType, companyId, template:"INV-{branch}-{yy}-{seq:6}", branchId?, gapless, resetPolicy}
#   POST /api/v1/numbering/preview | /allocate {documentType, companyId, branchId?, date, documentId}
#   PUT  /api/v1/numbering/series/{id}/counter {periodKey, nextNumber, reason?}; GET .../allocations; GET .../gaps
# platform: GET /api/v1/platform/jobs?state=&type= | /jobs/{id} | /jobs/types; POST /jobs/{id}/retry | /cancel
#   PUT  /api/v1/platform/schedules {code, jobType:"organization.rates.import", cron:"0 6 * * *", timeZone:"Asia/Baghdad", payload:{provider:"ecb"}}
#   operators: GET /api/v1/platform/ops/outbox?state=dead; POST /platform/ops/outbox/{id}/retry
# worker: dotnet run --project src/Host/Quicker.Worker  (or make up; health at http://localhost:8081/health/ready)
# idempotency: send Idempotency-Key: <uuid> on any POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE; a replay answers with Idempotent-Replayed: true
# webhooks: POST /api/v1/integration/webhooks {name, url, eventTypes:["sales.*"]} → {subscription, secret} (secret shown once)
#   POST .../webhooks/{id}/test; GET .../webhooks/{id}/deliveries; POST .../webhooks/deliveries/{id}/replay; POST .../webhooks/{id}/rotate-secret
#   verify: HMAC-SHA256(secret, X-Quicker-Timestamp + "." + body) == X-Quicker-Signature (v1=hex)
# notifications: GET /api/v1/collaboration/notifications?unreadOnly=&limit=&before= | /unread-count; POST .../{id}/read | /read-all
#   PUT  /api/v1/collaboration/notifications/preferences [{kind:"*"|"system.announcement", inApp, email}]; POST .../announce {title:{en,ar}, body:{en,ar}, link}
#   email: Quicker__Email__Provider=smtp with SmtpHost/SmtpPort (Mailpit: http://localhost:8025); storage: Quicker__Storage__Provider=s3 (MinIO console http://localhost:9001)
# attachments: POST /api/v1/collaboration/attachments (multipart: entityType, entityId, file); GET ...?entityType=&entityId=; GET .../{id}/content; DELETE .../{id}
# lists: ?limit=50&cursor=<nextCursor> on paged lists; ?filter=country eq 'IQ' and cf.region eq 'north' and code in ('A','B') on companies; ?fields=id,code; ?expand=branches
#   validators: every GET carries an ETag (If-None-Match → 304); GET /organization/companies/{id} gives a version ETag for If-Match on PUT (412 when stale)
# accounting: GET /api/v1/accounting/chart-templates; POST /api/v1/accounting/charts/from-template {templateCode:"IFRS_SME"|"GCC"|"IRAQ_UAS", code, companyId?}
#   GET  /api/v1/accounting/charts/{id}?expand=accounts; POST .../charts/{id}/accounts {code, name:{en,ar}, type, parentCode, isControl, subledgerType, defaultRole, currencyRestriction}
#   PUT  /api/v1/accounting/accounts/{id}/dimension-rules [{dimensionCode:"COST_CENTER", rule:"required"|"optional"|"blocked", defaultValueId?}]
#   POST /api/v1/accounting/accounts/{id}/check {companyId, dimensions:{COST_CENTER: valueId}, currency, manual}  (what the posting engine would say)
#   GET  .../charts/{id}/export (CSV); POST .../charts/{id}/import (text/csv or {accounts:[...]}); GET|PUT .../charts/{id}/mappings/IRAQ_UAS
#   GET  /api/v1/accounting/companies/{id}/settings; PUT .../companies/{id}/chart {chartId}
#   POST /api/v1/accounting/companies/{id}/posting-profiles/from-chart; GET /posting-profiles/{id}; PUT .../rules [{accountRole, accountCode, documentType?, itemPostingGroupId?, branchId?}]; POST .../activate
#   POST /api/v1/accounting/postings {companyId, sourceModule, sourceDocumentType, sourceDocumentId, postingDate, currency, lines:[{accountRole:"AR", amount:100.01, subledgerType:"AR", subledgerRef}, {accountRole:"Revenue", amount:-100.01}], idempotencyKey?}
#   GET  /api/v1/accounting/journal-entries/{id}; POST .../{id}/reverse {reason, reversalDate?}; GET /companies/{id}/journal-entries?from=&to=&limit=&cursor=
#   GET  /api/v1/accounting/companies/{id}/balances; GET .../balances/verify; POST .../balances/rebuild   (or: dotnet run --project src/Host/Quicker.Migrator -- rebuild-balances --tenant demo)
#   POST /api/v1/accounting/companies/{id}/journals {kind:"manual"|"opening"|"accrual", postingDate, currency, description:{en,ar}, autoReverseOn?, lines:[{accountCode:"6110", debit:1500000}, {accountCode:"2170", credit:1500000}]}
#   POST /api/v1/accounting/journals/{id}/submit | /approve | /reject {reason} | /cancel | /post; GET /companies/{id}/journals?status=&from=&to=; PUT /organization/companies/{id}/settings/accounting.journals.approval {value:"required"}
#   POST /api/v1/accounting/companies/{id}/journals/import (text/csv: journal_ref,posting_date,currency,account_code,debit,credit,description | {journals:[...]})
#   POST /api/v1/accounting/companies/{id}/recurring-templates {code, name:{en,ar}, cron:"0 0 1 * *", currency, startsOn, amountMode:"fixed"|"percentage"|"variable", baseAmount?, requiresReview, lines}; POST /recurring-templates/{id}/generate {runDate?, baseAmount?}
#   POST /api/v1/accounting/companies/{id}/deferrals {kind:"prepayment", balanceAccountCode:"1410", targetAccountCode:"6110", startsOn, periods:12, totalAmount, currency, method:"straight_line"|"daily"}; POST .../deferrals/preview; POST /deferrals/{id}/post-due?asOf= | /cancel
#   POST /api/v1/accounting/routines/run?companyId=&asOf=   (auto-reversals, recurring journals, deferral lines; the schedule accounting.daily runs it at 00:30 UTC)
# integrity: POST /api/v1/platform/integrity/run?companyId=   (the invariant harness: entries balanced, trial balance zero, balances = lines, audit chain, isolation, gapless numbering; the schedule integrity.daily runs it at 03:30 UTC over every tenant and fails on any problem)
#   periods: PUT /api/v1/organization/periods/{id}/states {companyId, modules:["GL"], state:"hard_closed", reason}; POST .../periods/{id}/reopen {companyId, modules, reason, state} (accounting.period.reopen, recent authentication)
#   GET|PUT /api/v1/organization/companies/{id}/posting-windows [{roleId?, allowFrom?, allowTo?, reason?}]   (the engine refuses posting.outside_window)
#   inquiry: GET /api/v1/accounting/companies/{id}/reports/trial-balance?asOf=&from=&compareAsOf=&basis=fc|rc&groupBy=COST_CENTER&d.COST_CENTER=<valueId>&format=csv|xlsx
#   GET  /api/v1/accounting/companies/{id}/reports/ledger?accountCode=6110&from=&to=&limit=&cursor=&format=   (rows carry sourceLink to the document)
#   GET  /api/v1/accounting/companies/{id}/reports/dimension-balances?dimension=COST_CENTER&asOf=&accountType=expense; GET .../journal-entries?number=&accountId=&text=&isManual=&minAmount=
#   corrections: POST /api/v1/accounting/journals/{id}/correct {reason} → a draft in the first open period; post it to reverse the original there and post the replacement, linked both ways
#   POST /api/v1/accounting/journal-entries/{id}/correct {reason, replacement:{...posting request}}   (documents; reversal + replacement in one transaction)
# records: POST /api/v1/collaboration/comments {entityType, entityId, body, mentions:[membershipId], parentId?}; GET /activities?entityType=&entityId=
#   POST /api/v1/collaboration/links {from:{type,id}, to:{type,id}, relation:"source"}; GET /views?entityType=; POST /views {entityType, name, definition:{filters,sort,columns}, shared, isDefault}
#   POST /api/v1/collaboration/custom-fields {entityType:"company", key, label:{en,ar}, type:"select", required, options:[{value,label}], rules:{min,max,maxLength,pattern}, indexed}
#   GET  /api/v1/collaboration/custom-fields/company/schema; companies then take customFields:{...} on POST/PUT and echo them back
```

## How to resume in a new session

1. Read `CLAUDE.md`, then this file, then `docs/ROADMAP.md` for the current milestone and slice.
2. Re-read `docs/PRODUCT_BRIEF.md` once per session; skim `docs/ASSUMPTIONS.md` for anything marked `changed`.
3. Consult the ADR that governs the area you are about to touch before writing code.
4. Work in small commits; update this file and the relevant docs in the same commit as the code.
5. At the end of a milestone, write the report to the founder (built, how to see it, test results, decisions, gaps, next) and pause for review.
